- **scout**: Relay Scout — read-only recon sub-agent for the Planner. Explores the code relevant to a task and establishes the project's build/test/lint commands, then returns a context pack. - **scout**: Read-only exploration agent. Runs before the implementer to surface the relevant files, patterns, and constraints so the implementer starts with concrete context. ([em-jones/ems-harness](https://github.com/em-jones/ems-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md) · 2 copies)
- **scout**: Cheap read-only explorer on Haiku. Use for broad codebase searches, "where is X / how does Y work" questions, and reading many files, when only the conclusion is needed back. - **scout**: Cheapest tier (Haiku, low effort, read-only). Use for lookups, finding files or symbols, status reads, and bounded extraction where the answer is a fact you can check at a glance. Not for implementation, synthesis across sources, review, audit or any judgment call. ([EONRaider/agent-router](https://github.com/EONRaider/agent-router/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Phase 0 of deep-research. Passive collector. ([epireve/coscientist](https://github.com/epireve/coscientist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scout.md) · 2 copies)
- **scout**: Read-only reconnaissance. Use for any search, lookup, or "where/how is X" question that needs no judgment — locating files, symbols, usages, config values, or summarizing how something works across a codebase. Returns concise findings with file:line references. The cheapest way to gather facts when the search is bounded, separable from your own working context, and not feeding a coupled investigation you own. ([ercgn/claude-orchestrator](https://github.com/ercgn/claude-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md))

- **scout**: Cheap read-only code locator. Returns a compact file:line map, not file dumps. ([EviIius/Token-Optimizer](https://github.com/EviIius/Token-Optimizer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Fast, cheap, read-only fact-finder for fan-out work - verifying a doc claim against code, locating exemplars or canonical shapes, mining git history, checking whether a path/pattern/tool exists. - **screener**: Exam Forge low-cost screener. Reads a question file without opening the PDF and catches defects visible in the text itself, such as source references, giveaway options, questions that cannot stand alone, repeats and wrong difficulty labels. Called only by the Exam Forge orchestrator. ([on-retryx/exam-forge](https://github.com/on-retryx/exam-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/screener.md) · ★1)
- **screener**: High-volume single-document work — classification, tagging, yes/no screening, single-field extraction from one short document. - **screener**: Cheap first pass on one claim - desk check, zero-GPU trace mining over existing runs, or an 8-task smoke screen (~0.8 GPU-h) - so dev-48 hours go only to ideas that survived.
